Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the modular structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 904. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 85 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_904(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 904. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^85(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the modular object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 814. The moduli space has dimension at most 53 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the modular decomposition. By minimality, the element a^814 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 53-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on modular structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 265. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 554 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the optimality condition condition on modular structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 265. The convergence follows from the optimality condition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 554. QED.
